Hi, Doctor. Thank you for seeing me. Am I good to sit right here? Okay, thank you. 

I, uh... I hope I'm not taking up time from someone who needs it more. It's just... well, mostly I just don't feel like myself anymore. I'm usually a guy who gets things done, you know? But recently, I've kind of just... stopped.

Um... focus on things, or make decisions? No, I don't think so. I mean, my job requires me to be pretty sharp, right? You have to be able to assess a situation and act fast. I've been doing this a long time, so making decisions is just second nature to me now.

Um... since the divorce? Well... I mean, I guess things have been a little... complicated since I moved into the smaller rental. That was right after the divorce was finalized, about a year and two months ago. 

I wouldn't say things are completely unraveling or anything bad like that! Just... you know, it's an adjustment. Going from a house to a smaller place, being on my own. It's been a little tough to keep everything perfectly organized and on track, but I'm really trying my best to stay on top of it all.

I'm working really hard to keep my life together and be a good employee. Does that sound like a normal way to handle it? I really hope I'm doing okay.

Um... when I find what? Like, when I find myself trying to make things perfect? 

Well... I mean, I wouldn't say it gets in the way of anything. I think it's actually a really good trait, especially in my job. You want to make sure you're doing things right the first time, right? I really try to make sure I'm meeting expectations, both at work and... well, here with you, too. I hope that makes sense? I'm not doing anything wrong by wanting to be thorough, right?
